<script type="text/javascript">
//Get Cookie
function getCookie(cname) {
    var name = cname + "=";
    var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
    for(var i=0; i<ca.length; i++) {
        var c = ca[i].trim();
           if (c.indexOf(name)==0) return c.substring(name.length,c.length);
    }
    return "";
}
//Associate Login
function getAssociate() {
	try { 
		return JSON.parse(unescape(getCookie('aos'))).assID;
	} catch(e){
		return '';
	}
}
function getQueryStrings() {
    var assoc = {};
    var decode = function(s) { return decodeURIComponent(s.replace(/\+/g, " ")); };
    var queryString = location.search.substring(1);
    var keyValues = queryString.split('&');

    for (var i in keyValues) {
        var key = keyValues[i].split('=');
        if (key.length > 1) {
            assoc[decode(key[0])] = decode(key[1]);
        }
    }
    try { 
    	return assoc.code;
	} catch(e){
		return '';
	}
}
function associateItemsWithShippingMethods(items, groupings) {
	var itemsWithShippingDetails = [];
	groupings.forEach(function(grouping) {
		grouping.orderItemIds.forEach(function(orderItemId) {
				itemsWithShippingDetails.push(grouping.shippingMethodName);
		});
	});
	return itemsWithShippingDetails;
}

	'page_type': 'home page'	
	 	
};

<script type="text/javascript">
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
    var ngNow = require.mozuData('pagecontext').now;
    require(['modules/jquery-mozu', 'slick', 'moment', 'modules/moment-tz', 'gtag'], function($, slick, moment, gtag) {
        // Once the document is loaded
        $(document).ready(function() {
            
            //for new searchbox 
            if( $( '.searchBoxTrigger' ) ){
                $( '.searchBoxTrigger' ).on('click', function(){
                    $('.searchBoxWrapper').css({'top': 0, 'visibility': 'visible'});

                    $('#closeSearch').on('click', function(){
                        $('.searchBoxWrapper').css({'top': -111111, 'visibility': 'hidden'});
                    });
                });
            }


            //for about us email form submission
            //https://offers.christmastreeshops.com/SignupWidget/dynamic/CTSUS_email_offers?aqu_source=1180&email=jdelatorre312@gmail.com&ZipCode=07502
            if( $("#aboutSignUp") ){
                $("#aboutSignUp").click(function (e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                        var email = $("#aboutUsEmail").val();
                        var zip = $("#aboutUsZip").val();

                        var re = /^(([^<>()\[\]\\.,;:\s@"]+(\.[^<>()\[\]\\.,;:\s@"]+)*)|(".+"))@((\[[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\])|(([a-zA-Z\-0-9]+\.)+[a-zA-Z]{2,}))$/;
                        
                        if (re.test(email.toLowerCase())) {
                            $("#emailHelpBlockAbout").text("");

                        }

            // FEATURED CATEGORY AND SHOP BY CATEGORY SECTION FUNCTIONS
            function categorySetup(thisSearch, slotId, subCat, featuredSection) {
                // Collect date and content data
                var startDate = moment.tz(thisSearch.find(".startDate:first").val(), "America/New_York");
                var endDate = moment.tz(thisSearch.find(".endDate:first").val(), "America/New_York");
                var sectionCounter = 1;
                // If the current date is between the start and end date as you loop
                if (startDate <= curDate && curDate < endDate) {

                    // Set the content based on what was collected above
                    var featured01html = '';

                    thisSearch.find(subCat).each(function() {
                        var imgSrc = $(this).find(".imgSrc:first").val();
                        var infoText = $(this).find(".featuredname:first").val();
                        var hrefUrl = $(this).find(".featuredlink:first").val();

                        featured01html += '<div class="col-sm-4 margin-bottom-15">';
                        featured01html += '<a href="' + hrefUrl + '?ab=hp-' + featuredSection + sectionCounter + '" class="">';

                        featured01html += '<img src="' + imgSrc + '" alt="' + infoText + '" class="img-thumbnail margin-bottom-15"><br>';
                        featured01html += '<span style="font-size: 24px; color: #0eb1c9;">' + infoText + '</span></a></div>';
                        ++sectionCounter;
                    });

                    $(slotId).html(featured01html);

                    // exit the loop
                    return false;
                }
            }

            // FEATURED CATEGORY AND SHOP BY CATEGORY SECTION FUNCTIONS
            function shopBySetup(thisSearch, slotId, subCat, featuredSection) {
                // Collect date and content data
                var startDate = moment.tz(thisSearch.find(".startDate:first").val(), "America/New_York");
                var endDate = moment.tz(thisSearch.find(".endDate:first").val(), "America/New_York");
                var sectionCounter = 1;
                // If the current date is between the start and end date as you loop
                if (startDate <= curDate && curDate < endDate) {

                    // Set the content based on what was collected above
                    var featured01html = '';
                    featured01html += '<div class="row">';

                    thisSearch.find(subCat).each(function() {
                        var imgSrc = $(this).find(".imgSrc:first").val();
                        var infoText = $(this).find(".featuredname:first").val();
                        var hrefUrl = $(this).find(".featuredlink:first").val();

                        featured01html += '<div class="col-sm-4 margin-bottom-15">';
                        featured01html += '<a href="' + hrefUrl + '?ab=hp-' + featuredSection + sectionCounter + '" class="">';

                        featured01html += '<img src="' + imgSrc + '" alt="' + infoText + '" class="img-thumbnail margin-bottom-15"><br>';
                        featured01html += '<span style="font-size: 24px; color: #0eb1c9;">' + infoText + '</span></a></div>';

                        if (sectionCounter == 3) {
                            featured01html += '</div><div class="row">';
                        } else if (sectionCounter == 6) {
                            featured01html += '</div>';
                        }
                        ++sectionCounter;
                    });

                    $(slotId).html(featured01html);

                    // exit the loop
                    return false;
                }
            }

                console.log("hero slider function");
                // COLLECT DATES
                var startDate = moment.tz($(this).find(".startDate:first").val(), "America/New_York");
                var endDate = moment.tz($(this).find(".endDate:first").val(), "America/New_York");

                //IF DATE WITHIN RANGE, START BUILDING SLIDER
                if (startDate <= curDate && curDate < endDate) {
                    console.log("hero slider entering info function");
                    var herohtml = '';
                    var mobilehtml = '';
                    var herocounter = 1;

                    //FOR EACH SLIDE, GET IMG SOURCE, LINK, AND ALT TAG
                    $(this).find(".slide").each(function() {
                        var imgSrc = $(this).find(".imgSrc:first").val();
                        var mobileImg = $(this).find(".mobileSrc:first").val();
                        var infoText = $(this).find(".featuredname:first").val();
                        var hrefUrl = $(this).find(".featuredlink:first").val();
                        var order = $(this).find(".featuredorder:first").val();
                        var whiteBoxHeader = $(this).find(".boxHeader").val();
                        var whiteBoxCopy = $(this).find(".boxCopy").val();
                        var whiteBoxCtaText = $(this).find(".ctaText").val();
                        var whiteBoxBg = $(this).find(".ctaColor").val();
                        var whiteBoxDate = $(this).find(".featureddate").val();
                        var whiteBoxPrice = $(this).find(".boxPrice").val();
                        var whiteBoxTag = $(this).find(".boxTag").val();

                        
                        herohtml += '<div class="swiper-slide"><a href="' + hrefUrl + '?ab=hp-slide' + herocounter + '">';
                        herohtml += '<img src="' + imgSrc + '" alt="' + infoText + '" >';
                        herohtml += '<div class="whitebox font-switch" style="background-color: ' + whiteBoxBg + '">';
                        
                        mobilehtml += '<div class="mobile-slide"><a href="' + hrefUrl + '?ab=hp-slide' + herocounter + '">';
                        mobilehtml += '<img src="' + mobileImg + '" alt="' + infoText + '" >';
                        mobilehtml += '<div class="whitebox font-switch" style="background-color: ' + whiteBoxBg + '">';
                        
                        if(whiteBoxDate != ''){
                            herohtml += '<div class="whiteBoxDate">' + whiteBoxDate + '</div>';
                            mobilehtml += '<div class="whiteBoxDate">' + whiteBoxDate + '</div>';
                        }

                        herohtml += '<h3>' + whiteBoxHeader + '</h3>';
                        herohtml += '<p>' + whiteBoxCopy + '</p>';
                        
                        mobilehtml += '<h3>' + whiteBoxHeader + '</h3>';
                        mobilehtml += '<p>' + whiteBoxCopy + '</p>';
                        
                        if (whiteBoxPrice != '') {
                            herohtml += '<div class="price">' + whiteBoxPrice + ' <span>' + whiteBoxTag + '</span></div>';
                            mobilehtml += '<div class="price">' + whiteBoxPrice + ' <span>' + whiteBoxTag + '</span></div>';
                        }

                        herohtml += '<span href="' + hrefUrl + '" >';
                        herohtml += whiteBoxCtaText + ' ></span>';
                        herohtml += '</div></a></div>';
                        
                        mobilehtml += '<span href="' + hrefUrl + '" >';
                        mobilehtml += whiteBoxCtaText + ' ></span>';
                        mobilehtml += '</div></a></div>';

                        ++herocounter;

                    });

                    //ADD SLIDES TO DESKTOP SLIDER    
                    $(".homepage-slider").html(herohtml);

                    //ADD SLIDES TO MOBILE SLIDER
                    $(".mobile-home-slider").html(mobilehtml);

                    //INITIATE SLIDER
                    $('.homepage-slider, .mobile-home-slider').slick({
                        infinite: true,
                        autoplay: true,
                        autoplaySpeed: 5000,
                        prevArrow: '<i class="fa fa-angle-left" aria-label="Previous Slide Link"></i>',
                        nextArrow: '<i class="fa fa-angle-right" aria-label="Next Slide Link"></i>'
                    });

                    $('.mobile-home-slider').addClass('activated');
                    

                    // EXIT THE LOOP
                    return false;
                }
